### Callback findings: mechanism, ownership and remaining gaps

- Exact screenshot provenance: third image in the owner message “record the external correlation against attempt 03”, the full HitPay charge view. The lower-right panel beside Notes shows **Webhook / Failed** and separately **Sent at Oct. 08, 2026, 1:00 AM**. This is a dashboard-reported sent time without explicit timezone or seconds. The upper-left Complete date is payment completion and is not its source.
- Actual notification destination, HTTP response status, HTTP Content-Type, retry outcome and root cause remain unavailable. The HTML body shows a meta-refresh and anchor targeting `app.sbpay.me`, redacted path `/return/[company]/hitpay/[redacted]`. That is a response redirect target, not proof of the request destination, HTTP 3xx or a Location header. A charset meta tag does not establish the HTTP Content-Type.
- The owner's later supplied-description assertion of payload `status: completed` and an `hmac` field is retained as supplied evidence; no separate raw payload file was located in the retained attempt receipts. Completed is payment status. An HMAC field does not establish signature verification or delivery acknowledgement.
- [HitPay Create Payment Request](https://docs.hitpayapp.com/apis/payment-request/create-request) distinguishes user `redirect_url` from server `webhook`. The [Online Payments guide](https://docs.hitpayapp.com/apis/guide/online-payments) recommends registered events and calls the per-request field deprecated; that does not prove which route this native plugin uses.
- The [HitPay Events guide](https://docs.hitpayapp.com/apis/guide/events), “Validating Webhook”, explicitly distinguishes older payment-request/charge/plugin callbacks: sorted and concatenated parameters signed with the API-key salt, carried in payload `hmac`; registered events instead sign raw JSON with a per-endpoint salt and use `Hitpay-Signature`. The reported shape is consistent with the older/plugin family, not sufficient to prove its configuration or exclude another integration. No salt is needed from YS for this research or experiment; do not request/export it.
- The registered-event guide's sample acknowledges with HTTP 200 and rejects invalid signatures with 401. That example is not a verified acknowledgement/body or retry contract for this native plugin callback. No applicable fixed retry count/schedule was established from the inspected primary sources, and no actual retry result is retained. Do not substitute another webhook mechanism's signature or retry rules.
- [SimplyBook's HitPay setup](https://help.simplybook.me/index.php?title=SBPay.me_payment_systems_setup) configures HitPay through SBPay's native processor settings. This supports the native connector as the expected integration owner, but actual receiver ownership cannot be proved without the destination. Nothing establishes our planned Cloudflare Worker as its receiver; no receiver deployment or callback replacement is proposed.
- Official forum [#1245](https://tech-support.simplybook.me/t/1245-book-function-client-id-invoice-creation-and-payment-webhook/1245) discusses invoice-enabled booking and a different processor's callback forwarding, not this HitPay receiver's acknowledgement. [#1489](https://tech-support.simplybook.me/t/1489-booking-change-cancel-triggers-not-calling-callback-url/1490) concerns SimplyBook booking-event hooks around payment confirmation, not HitPay-to-SBPay delivery. Neither establishes browser independence or authorizes settings changes.

Smallest useful further fact: the failed delivery's **original destination hostname/redacted route and HTTP result**, if exposed in its existing dashboard details. Do not repeatedly request screenshots of the same response panel. Closing a tab after success is not a control. The owner's Edge canary showed `Fetch.requestPaused` followed by `Fetch.continueRequest` and then DNS failure. It did not establish return prevention. No tenant wildcard, payment submission automation, network upload or server-to-server interception is included. Any unknown return destination, disabled control, other-profile return or missing final control evidence makes isolation inconclusive. Do not repeat a payment after an uncertain result.

Native dashboard reads use the planned read-only reconciliation; no receiver deployment or webhook resend is implied.

- **Browser-independent payment update observed:** owner evidence establishes HitPay success and continuous request-stage prevention, API verifies the same invoice paid with correct amount/association and final valid booking. External transaction correlation is tracked separately until matching references arrive.
- **Webhook delivery verified:** only separate delivery/receiver evidence establishes it; a paid invoice, completed payload or HMAC presence cannot.
- **Unresolved mismatch:** HitPay succeeded but invoice remains unpaid/cancels/disagrees or final booking is invalid. Preserve financial truth and reserve-read results for native review; reaching the poll limit does not prove the update can never occur.
- **Inconclusive:** return control is missing/bypassed, payment did not complete, authentication/transport/schema prevents observation or bounded observations end without enough evidence. Preserve any successful payment separately.

YS owns manual reconciliation: locate only the new exact marker/code/invoice, read native SBPay payment-system transaction ID, join it to HitPay sandbox charge/reference/amount/status, and record disposition/follow-up before ending the session. The assistant provides the private known-ID sheet. No automatic reinstate/cancel/refund/manual-paid, resend, replacement booking or provider-setting change. Missing IDs do not authorize broad discovery. No Git publication, merge or deployment in this preparation.

### Documentation-backed gaps

Official [public methods](https://help.simplybook.me/wiki/Company_public_service_methods) describe createBatch as returning an integer. The [official API index](https://be.simplybook.me/api/doc) exposes closeBatch without a parameter contract and marks it internal. [Dated staff guidance](https://tech-support.simplybook.me/t/575-multiple-products-added-when-booking-with-count/581) supplies three closeBatch arguments including a signature but does not establish this campaign's supported hash acquisition/recovery sequence. No batch was created. [2026 staff clarification](https://tech-support.simplybook.me/t/verifying-api-booking-flow-with-member-discount-via-promo-code/1763) distinguishes multiple sessions from group count; it does not prove atomicity or a safe cart maximum.

[Take Me Back Home](https://help.simplybook.me/index.php/Take_Me_Back_Home_custom_feature) documents a booking-page return feature; it does not prove native invoice-payment completion routing to our protected frontend. Current feature status is false and settings changes are excluded. Generic direct-HitPay sandbox Card/PayNow support is not native connector PayNow proof. Cloud browser controls used only synthetic loopback traffic in a network-disabled container; actual native return, popup coverage and provider notifications remain unproved.

M2.1–M2.5, CLOUD-01, P01–P12, client production, device acceptance and later milestones remain open to their precise gates. Offline contract/adapter work using these observed shapes can proceed; no production acceptance, contract freeze or release is implied.

An emitted chunk is rejected413; query parameters and incorrect origins remain refused. Customer cookies cannot authorize operator grants. `foundation_trace` is a structured internal log event with boundary, correlationId, outcome, optional HTTP status, total duration and numeric span counts/durations/errors. Allowed span names: storage, coordinator admission, coordinator shared wait, coordinator RPC envelope and provider transport. No URL/headers/cookies/capabilities/SQL/arguments/results/error text enters this record. AsyncLocalStorage isolates overlapping HTTP/RPC callers; the correlation ID is passed explicitly across RPC. Provider spans include the fixed15-second response deadline and bounded body parsing. The governor rejects overload rather than maintaining an application queue. RPC time includes platform transit/scheduling and must not be called isolated queue time. Shared wait and nested storage/admission spans overlap; no percentile or placement conclusion is claimed.

The accepted timing observer handles Cloudflare RPC thenables through settlement, not only native Promise instances; the enclosing-RPC assertion verifies this boundary. All provider operations remain disabled/end0 and existing operation grants expired.

### Experiment 1 — protected separate checkout (9 October 2026)

`ProtectedCheckoutService.context` reads the authenticated, owned attempt's summary and checking window from `GET /api/attempts/{id}/checkout`. It returns an explicit live/synthetic mode and available/unavailable checkout. Production currently always returns live/unavailable: no supported native preparation/URL contract or provider host allowlist has been established. The client rejects every live URL until that contract is reviewed. The isolated synthetic composition accepts only its exact attempt-bound relative path. Neither browser input/storage nor a query parameter supplies checkout authority; no live-to-demo fallback exists.

The original confirmation endpoint still uses the canonical decision owner, authenticated before the owned attempt read. Response JSON remains compatible; `X-Checking-Deadline`, `X-Next-Check`, and `X-Checks-Remaining` carry the protected checking window. An atomic D1 reservation permits at most one effective confirmation decision per configured polling interval (currently 5 seconds), with at most120 decisions per immutable attempt deadline. Concurrent/early requests receive429; exhausted requests receive429/zero remaining; elapsed windows return unresolved. Storage failure fails closed. Checking never calls a provider or runs recovery. Migration0007 adds only `confirmation_next_ms` and `confirmation_checks` to the existing attempt owner. Migration0006 and0001–0005 remain unchanged. A populated0006→0007 test retains intent, unknown effects, dispatch identities, recovery records, completed proofs and coordinator accounting.

`useConfirmationChecking` owns the mounted page's checking lifecycle: a synchronous busy guard, abort/generation fencing, hidden-page pause, one eligible foreground refresh, and a10-second request bound clipped to the remaining deadline. A browser lock coordinates atomic local reservations where supported; stored deadline/cooldown/count are only conservative optimizations. Server enforcement remains authoritative without locks/storage and across devices. Reload/manual clicks never extend a deadline or clear a count. Terminal UI results are not persisted as proof; clearing only the terminal browser cooldown permits a fresh protected authentication read on reload, still subject to the server's unchanged rate/count/deadline. ## Native adapter contracts — PR13 checkpoint, 9 October 2026

This section supersedes the absent-response and empty-initial-host gaps in the preceding preparation packet. It does not reopen the campaign. Source-only implementation builds on reviewed PR12; actual final SHA/checks/artifact are recorded in the new draft PR. Historical booking23 remains untouched.

### Provenance and supported normalization

Six user-supplied historical response bodies cover public book, signed booking before/final, Admin invoice before/after and `{link}`. They are not newly fetched responses or independently verified original bytes. Historical booking23 is associated to invoice23 through complete `lines[].booking_ids` and `lines[].bookings`, not merely equal IDs. The invoice changes number while retaining ID. Its new/false → paid/true transition names hitpay, SGD88/tax0; final booking remains confirmed. Creation `require_payment:false` is not payment evidence. Shared redaction markers do not establish matched original customer/account identity.

The private original is unchanged outside Git. `worker/tests/native-fixtures.json` is explicitly labelled and minimized, with different synthetic booking/invoice/line/customer/service/provider identities; no historical code, token, contact, processor capability or usable payment URL is committed. Controlled historical receipt clocks are test-only. The later owner message supplies only initial HTTPS origin `https://soccerbotstudiosg.simplybook.asia`, fixed path `/v2/client/pay-later/id/[REDACTED]/hash/[REDACTED]`, no query and no fragment. No supplied URL was contacted. `src/domain/native-checkout.ts` is the single shared initial-destination policy; no wildcard, independent HitPay link, URL construction or redirect-host inference.

`provider-normalization` accepts explicit boolean/0/1 representations, canonical positive IDs and exact decimal minor units (including trailing zero precision such as88.0000). Unknown flags, malformed/unsafe decimals, incomplete/extra/duplicate/foreign invoice associations and wrong customer/service/provider/amount/currency/tax are rejected. Only the evidenced single full-price, zero-tax, non-refunded/non-recurring profile is supported. Intent supplies price;88 is fixture data only. Paid plus invalid booking remains canonical unresolved, never confirmed. Unknown or contradictory payment states remain unknown. Invoice numbers are not identifiers.

Booking local wall times require the trusted Asia/Singapore contract. Start plus40minutes is play end; plus50minutes is occupied end, checked against returned start/end and duration/buffer fields. Null client timezone supplies no authority. Invoice creation/payment timestamps are not freshness receipts; due-time local guards use the separately retained rule below. Observation timestamps come from completed authenticated reads; creation and historical entities are never stamped as fresh confirmation. Existing15-second freshness remains unchanged.

### Named request, composition and read boundaries

### Concrete trial packet and remaining dependencies

Candidate fixture stays12October2026 09:00Asia/Singapore, service2/provider2, one player,40+10, SGD8800/tax0 only if the fresh trusted checks approve it and it remains at least48hours away. No alternative selection; no historical IDs reused. Use the already accepted isolated developer/merchant sandbox and owner-controlled contact by private reference; no repeated setup confirmation or payment23. Existing contact values are unavailable in this workspace and are not invented.

The complete book response can now supply pre-checkout booking/invoice association without the previous two extra reads. The mapped request envelope is **12**, not10:2 auth +1 company/info +1 required-fields +1 slot +1 book +1 native link +2 post-submission reads +2 reserved reconciliation reads +1 documented known-reference read reserve. This is2 above the proposed ceiling; without the general reserve it is11. Fresh price/entitlement/identity/auth refresh requirements could add operations and must be bound before authorization; no claim that12 currently guarantees an executable30-minute trial. Every physical failure/unknown dispatch counts. Owner browser/provider-internal traffic is separate and not falsely included.

After one owner-operated native sandbox submission, the supervised operator invokes the original attempt's initial readback phase once when due. A second pair is an explicit reserve action, never automatic. Actual per-response freshness governs canonical confirmed; stale/unknown/conflicting/paid-invalid evidence yields unresolved/review with no booking/payment replay, cancellation, refund or replacement. Manual return does not prove autonomous no-return recovery. Owner submission must be before the minimum of grant end, verified invoice due minus30seconds and handoff+10minutes. Unknown expiry withholds the link.

Remaining concrete execution dependencies: (1) approved private contact, persisted player mapping and current pre-write response mappings, (2) permitted redirect-chain/merchant binding for the trial, (3) secure deployed signing binding and separately reviewed finite native write-grant/operator composition, (4) an auth/identity lifetime plan consistent with the current short retention and amended request ceiling. The supplied bodies, initial path and retained request-builder/local expiry rules resolve those parsing/initial-host/request-shape/guard gaps; they do not resolve these items. No complete payment URL, credentials, personal values, repeat account installation or historical payment is requested. The release is reviewable closed source, not yet a runnable native trial; one consolidated remote authorization request remains premature.

Retained local expiry evidence: invoice created2026-10-08 00:58:30, due01:08:30 interpreted+08:00; attempt03 submission cutoff01:08:00SGT. Missing/unparseable due or now>=due stops checkout. Before generation require180seconds to min(due, supervision deadline); submission stops at min(supervision deadline, handoff+10minutes, due−30seconds). This observed ten-minute interval is not a provider TTL or evidence of cancellation/payment failure. `prepareNativeLink` takes the selected invoice ID and due value from trusted server composition, verifies the association and stores the derived cutoff; customer input cannot set these.

## Dynamic catalogue and pre-booking — current contract

This section supersedes current-status10/12/14-call estimates above; those remain historical proposal evidence. YS maintains routine data in SimplyBook. No source edit is needed to parse supported added/renamed/deactivated providers, service names/prices or offered schedules. No four-instructor runtime limit; names/response order never choose identity. Customer Preview remains separate and is not silently switched to live.

Supported documented requests added to the existing fixed transport: `getEventList(true,false)`, `getUnitList(true,false)`, `getAvailableUnits(serviceId, "YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss",1)`. Existing `getAdditionalFields(serviceId)` and exact-date `getStartTimeMatrix(date,date,serviceId,providerId,1)` are reused. Sources: [official public methods](https://help.simplybook.me/wiki/Company_public_service_methods) and [official API guide/examples](https://simplybook.me/en/api/developer-api/ref). Documentation is request/schema evidence, not a new provider-account observation. Full-map APIs have no page/cursor arguments; unknown wrappers/cursors or oversized responses reject. No silent first-page assumption or undocumented endpoint. Flat unit_map is supported; provider-specific-duration/object mappings reject until documented and implemented. Each physical request still reserves through the existing coordinator, including errors/unknown outcomes.

`ProviderSession.catalogue` owns shared catalogue reads through its account-bound session. `CatalogueReads` validates account/environment/company, coalesces pending reads, clones returns and retains at most8 scopes for60seconds. The existing response-size bound also applies. Fresh pre-write reads bypass/invalidate cache; late superseded reads cannot repopulate it. Native write boundaries invalidate before dispatch and on completion/error. Selected-slot/required-field/eligibility checks are not cached across writes; their request includes exact service/provider/date/time as appropriate. Names never form cache keys. Cached catalogue display is not write authority or a reservation.

`ProviderSession.prebooking` now composes a concrete `NativePrebooking` instance used by the native adapter's revalidate boundary. It checks immutable account/environment, canonical IDs, single-session profile, trial48-hour lead before/after reads, current active relationship, exact price/currency, empty supported required fields, current eligible units and offered start, and all per-response receipt freshness (15seconds). It never accepts browser catalogue/clock/money as authority. Provider entity timestamps do not supply freshness. Missing or changed slot/provider returns selection_unavailable; changed price is returned with review_required and current display data. The shared frontend catalogue-review helper also detects renames against the prior display snapshot. Review preserves input/IDs; the customer must approve a fresh intent before commitment, rather than mutating a prepared attempt. Committed journal rows are immutable and retain original amounts/IDs after renames/deactivation. Current frontend catalogue boundary is validated but returns unavailable without an authorized route; no guessed HTTP endpoint or demo fallback.

Actual unresolved checks remain explicit, and `revalidate` refuses dispatch rather than accepting a callback assertion: supported provider-customer binding for the controlled contact; current tax-inclusive quote semantics (catalogue price does not establish zero tax); native player persistence; shared-studio binding/authoritative cross-instructor capacity; and assigned-instructor policy when multiple units are eligible. Existing Caelan-over-Nabil priority remains business policy, not name matching or response order; no unverified developer ID mapping is installed. Configure actual assigned schedules in SimplyBook; if overlaps remain, bind the approved priority by native IDs before auto-assignment. A second instructor does not create a second studio.

Player gap: historical getAdditionalFields(2)=[] proves no established player field. Every player count, including1, remains blocked for native commitment. Required input is the actual native Number of players field identifier/type/options, supported book.additional representation and authoritative persisted readback; or an explicit scoped business decision revising the requirement. No notes-field guess, silent one-player substitution or count>1. Initial checkout path/request-builder/expiry rules are already resolved and are not requested again. Downstream sandbox routing and deployed signing binding remain separate. Do not send secrets or usable checkout URLs.

### Finite authentication and operation budget — current proposal

Do not stretch5-second identity or60-second token retention across the30-minute owner window. Use separate explicit server-supervised phases with fresh bounded authentication as needed; no automatic refresh, retries or recovery-reserve borrowing. Existing identity path costs up to4 calls from cold state: public getToken, Admin auth, company/info, tariff/current. Previous estimates omitted tariff and subsequent phase authentication. Keep its existing verification rather than removing a check to fit a target. An identity-expired phase stops before another target request; a cold/expired later phase requires its separately reserved setup. The5-second native readback deadline remains, and the pre-write phase must stop if current identity/admission expires before booking/link. This may stop a slow run; it does not promise30minutes of continuous authority.

| Phase / purpose                                  | Exact known target operations                                                                                    | Setup maximum | Target maximum | Known ceiling |
| ------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------: | -------------: | ------------: |
| Pre-write and handoff, one attempted sequence    | services list; instructors list; required fields; eligible units; exact slot; one book; one invoice payment-link |             4 |              7 |            11 |
| Owner returns; original attempt initial readback | signed getBookingDetails; known Admin invoice                                                                    |             4 |              2 |             6 |
| Explicit reserve readback only when needed/due   | same known-reference pair, no writes                                                                             |             4 |              2 |             6 |
| Named known-reference general read reserve       | one predeclared booking or invoice read                                                                          |             4 |              1 |             5 |
| Total known operations                           | 12 target operations plus up to16 phase setup calls                                                              |            16 |             12 |            28 |

**28 is a conditional known-operation ceiling, not a complete executable approval request.** It is16 above the previous12-call proposal and18 above the original10. Reusing still-valid tokens may reduce actual calls; unused setup does not authorize additional operations. Mandatory unresolved customer/quote/player/capacity/assignment checks may add `U` documented operations and their bounded setup; the complete trial ceiling is not fixed until `U` is bound. No invented zero-cost validation. General reserve can be omitted only by an explicit revised plan; phase recovery capacity stays separate. The current8/80 usage and16-call campaign recovery reserve remain unchanged. Ordinary known phases use16 calls and two recovery phases12. The currently exposed provider control admits ordinary reads only; wiring a separately granted native recovery phase to the reserved admission path remains part of the finite operator composition, not an activated capability. Validate that accounting for the final plan before approval. Owner browser/provider-internal traffic is separate. All dispatched failures/unknowns count; stop without replay/cancel/refund/replacement.

The remaining execution window is one proposed30-minute operation, one accepted single-session booking and one owner-operated native sandbox submission, with approved controlled contact and eligible fresh slot. The previous12October09:00 service2/provider2/SGD88 candidate remains conditional and is not a fallback: read current data and stop if different, late or unavailable. A newly approved review can use different supported IDs/prices without source edits, but never silently substitutes inside the original attempt. Submission cutoff remains min(supervision end, actual due−30seconds, handoff+10minutes), with180seconds before min(due,supervision) required before generation. Unknown/invalid/elapsed due withholds checkout; no cancellation/payment-failure inference.

Release stages stay separate: closed release up to36 management calls (182→218 proposed cumulative,18 beyond current200); closed deployment plus code rollback uses2 remaining deployment slots. Admission enablement and closing each need another deployment with current configuration (proposed cumulative14 rather than12) and an additional management envelope not yet bound. Trial provider dispatches are separately28+U, not management calls. Verification and rollback reserves are retained. No remote action/allowance increase is authorized, and no consolidated execution question is appropriate until the missing implementation/contracts and all stage ceilings are concrete.

### Native configuration proposals — not applied

**Player field:** reuse an existing valid field if present; otherwise enable Intake Forms only if entitled and separately approved. Create one required dropdown “Number of players”, choices `1,2,3,4`, no default, attached to service2. Keep `count=1`. Capture the generated identifier; later book uses `{handle_invoice:true, [actualIdentifier]: String(reviewedPlayers)}` in params[5], with contact/binding in params[4]. This is documented request representation, not the current strict adapter's accepted schema. Implement only after identifier/type/options are observed. Invoice inclusion is optional and requires an explicit decision; do not change email templates. Rollback: close SoccerBot admission, detach/disable only the newly created field if no accepted booking depends on it; preserve historical field answers and prior settings. If used, leave its definition/history intact and stop new consumption rather than delete evidence. Verify persistence on the one later authorized booking; do not create a second “test” booking automatically.

**Shared studio:** if absent, propose Related Resources group “Studio”, **Same number as clients**, one item representing the sole studio, attached to every service occupying it, each booking allocation count1 and each instructor capacity1. This mode is selected because the documented “Only one” class mode can allow multiple bookings in the same slot. Service Time details40+10; end-of-day-without-buffer off. Owner must approve the affected service list and feature entitlement; no paid upgrade or resources/schedules changed now. Rollback restores captured links/settings only after resolving new bookings, and closes admission first; never delete a resource with unresolved reservations. Public documentation describes automatic allocation but does not prove buffer locks this account's resource: native conflict acceptance remains required.

The probe is `ProviderSession.discoverFields`, using only providerRequest through the existing coordinator. Stop on wrong account/binding, expired grant/identity/token, incomplete setup,401/403/challenge, redirect, unknown/malformed/oversized representation, duplicate field identifiers, changed accounting, active unknown reservations, admission denial or timing limit. No bookings, edits/cancellations, historical23 reads, cart creation, payment-link requests, payment/refund, customer scans or provider settings are included.
